            function initViewPort() {
                if (navigator.userAgent.match(/Android|BlackBerry|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Opera Mini|IEMobile/i)) {
                    var viewport = document.getElementById("viewport"),
                        exist = true,
                        ori = window.orientation,
                        w = (ori==90 || ori==-90) ? screen.height : screen.width;

                    if(!viewport) {
                        exist = false;
                        viewport = document.createElement("meta");
                        viewport.name = "viewport";
                        viewport.id = "viewport";
                    }

                    if (w < 980) {
                        viewport.content = "width=device-width, initial-scale=0.75";
                    } else if (w > 980) {
                        viewport.content = "width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0";
                    }

                    if(!exist) {
                        document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0].appendChild(viewport);
                    }

                }
            }

            window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function() {
                initViewPort();
            });

            initViewPort();
        </script>

class="c-cookies" id="cookies-content" style="display: block;"><p class="c-header" lang="en">This website uses cookies</p><p class="c-message" lang="en">The website of Bax Music uses cookies and other similar techniques. The necessary cookies are used to enable basic functions while navigating our website and provide you with an optimal user experience. We also would like to use cookies to measure and analyse your interaction with the website, to improve both its functionality and your shopping experience.<br/><br/>Would you prefer not to use cookies? You can navigate to  <a href="javascript:showCookieSettings();">cookie preferences</a> to indicate which cookies we may and may not use. More information on cookies and the use of personal data by Bax Music can be found in our <a href="https://www.bax-shop.co.uk/privacy-policy">privacy policy</a>.</p><a class="c-button" href="javascript:void(0)" id="AcceptReload" lang="en" onclick="CookieConsent.dialog.submitConsent()"><span class="c-checkmark"></span>OK</a><a class="c-link" href="javascript:void(0)" id="edit-settings" lang="en" onclick="showCookieSettings()">Settings</a></div><div class="c-settings" id="settings-content" style="display: none;"><p class="c-header" lang="en">Settings</p><p class="c-message" lang="en">These cookies are used by Bax Music to provide you with an optimal user experience.</p><div class="c-content"><div class="cookieBannerCheckboxItemCheck"><label class="c-label disabled" lang="en">Necessary <input checked="checked" class="disabled" disabled="disabled" id="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonNecessary" name="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonNecessary" type="checkbox"/><span class="checkmark"></span></label></div><span class="c-intro" lang="en">Necessary cookies help make a website usable by enabling basic functions like page navigation and access to secure areas of the website. The website cannot function properly without these cookies.</span><div class="cookieBannerCheckboxItemCheck"><label class="c-label" for="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonPreferences" lang="en">Preferences <input checked="checked" id="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonPreferences" name="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonPreferences" type="checkbox"/><span class="checkmark"></span></label></div><span class="c-intro" lang="en">Preference cookies enable a website to remember information that changes the way the website behaves or looks, like your preferred language or the region that you are in.</span><div class="cookieBannerCheckboxItemCheck"><label class="c-label" lang="en">Statistics <input checked="checked" id="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonStatistics" name="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonStatistics" type="checkbox"/><span class="checkmark"></span></label></div><span class="c-intro" lang="en">Statistic cookies help website owners to understand how visitors interact with websites by collecting and reporting information anonymously.</span><div class="cookieBannerCheckboxItemCheck"><label class="c-label" lang="en">Marketing <input checked="true" id="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonMarketing" name="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonMarketing" type="checkbox"/><span class="checkmark"></span></label></div><span class="c-intro" lang="en">Marketing cookies are used to track visitors across websites. The intention is to display ads that are relevant and engaging for the individual user and thereby more valuable for publishers and third party advertisers.</span></div><div class="c-footer"><a class="c-button" href="javascript:void(0)" id="AcceptReload" lang="en" onclick="CookieConsent.dialog.submitConsent()"><span class="c-checkmark"></span>Save cookie preferences</a></div></div><div style="clear:both"></div></div>
